### Hard Audit Results:

1. **Disjunction Validation (P or Q)**:
   - **P**: Colonel Mustard is in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.
   - **Q**: Mrs. Peacock is in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.
   - Both P and Q are true, so this does not violate the disjunction rule.

2. **Biconditional Audit (P if and only if Q)**:
   - **P**: If Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM, then the Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.
   - **Q**: If the Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM, then Mr. Boddy was in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.
   - Both statements are logically consistent as the Lead Pipe was indeed in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM, and Mr. Boddy was there as well.

3. **Contradiction Search**:
   - **Col. Mustard**: 
     - 11:00 PM: Cloak Room (with the Lead Pipe).
     - 12:00 AM: Trophy Room.
   - **Mrs. Peacock**: 
     - 12:00 AM: Cloak Room.
   - **Mr. Boddy**: 
     - 12:00 AM: Trophy Room.
   - The movement of Colonel Mustard and Mrs. Peacock do not contradict any other clue. Silent movement or not explicitly contradicting a clue is common and not a fatal flaw.

4. **Manhattan Falsification**:
   - Movement from Cloak Room to Trophy Room: 
     - Coordinates from Cloak Room (2,0) to Trophy Room (1,1).
     - Calculation: \( \mathbf{|2-1| + |0-1| = 2} \)
     - Since 2 is greater than 1, this is a **PHYSICAL VIOLATION DETECTED**.

5. **Alone Test**:
   - Murder scene: Trophy Room at 12:00 AM.
   - Verify if any other suspect was present:
     - Miss Scarlet: Not in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM (Studio).
     - Monsieur Brunette: Not in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM (Billiard Room).
   - Mr. Boddy and Colonel Mustard were alone in the Trophy Room at 12:00 AM, and the Lead Pipe was there as well.

### Final Verdict:

The logical branch provided by the Detective is **REJECTED** due to a **PHYSICAL VIOLATION** during the movement from the Cloak Room to the Trophy Room. This disobeys the Manhattan distance rule.

The initial coordinates for the rooms are as follows:
- **Library**: (0, 0)
- **Fountain**: (1, 0)
- **Cloak Room**: (2, 0)
- **Billiard Room**: (0, 1)
- **Trophy Room**: (1, 1)
- **Studio**: (2, 1)

### Step 2: Initial Suspect and Weapon Status

Initially, we assume all suspects are in the Library (0, 0), and all weapons are in the Library (0, 0). We will update these as we process each clue.

### Step 3: Process the Clues

#### Clue 1: Mrs. Peacock and Professor Plum were in the Studio together at least once.
- This is a disjoint statement, meaning either both are in the Studio at the same time or at some other point.
- Let's set initial position: \( (2, 1) \) for professor and peacock.

#### Clue 2: Monsieur Brunette was in the Billiard Room at 11:30 PM.
- Position at 11:30 PM: \( (0, 1) \).

#### Clue 3: The Lead Pipe and Miss Scarlet were in the Cloak Room together at least once.
- Set initial position: \( (2, 0) \) for Miss Scarlet and Lead Pipe.

#### Clue 4: The Poison was in the same room as Monsieur Brunette at 12:30 AM.
- Position at 12:30 AM: Late position needs to be matched with Billiard Room = \( (0, 1) \).

#### Clue 5: Mr. Boddy and Colonel Mustard were at the Fountain together at least once.
- Set initial position: \( (1, 0) \) for both Boddy and Colonel Mustard.

#### Clue 6: The Poison was in the room just west of Professor Plum at 12:30 AM.
- If professor is at \( (2, 1) \), poison is at \( (2 - 1, 1) = (1, 1) \). Check if poison is in Trophy Room at previous time slots.

1. **Disjunction Validation (P or Q):**
   - No disjunctions to verify directly from the given clues.

2. **Biconditional Audit (P if and only if Q):**
   - **Clue 7**: "The Horseshoe was at the Fountain at 11:00 PM if and only if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM."
     - Checked: If the poison is in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM, the Horseshoe was indeed at the Fountain.
     - If the poison is not in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM, the Horseshoe is not at the Fountain.
     - Conclusion: No contradiction.

   - **Clue 16**: "Professor Plum was in the Studio at 11:00 PM or the Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM."
     - Checked: If the Lead Pipe is in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM, Professor Plum was indeed in the Studio.
     - If the Lead Pipe is not in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM, then Professor Plum can be in any room.
     - Conclusion: No contradiction.

   - **Clue 6**: "The Poison was in the room just west of Professor Plum at 12:30 AM."
     - Professor Plum at \( (2, 1) \) at 12:30 AM.
     - The west room is \( (1, 1) \) (Continental Room, not provided in initial list).

     - Horseshoe at \( (2, 0) \) at 12:00 AM.

     - **Conclusion:** No contradiction.
   
   - **Clue 13**: "The Candlestick was in the Library at 11:00 PM or a suspect motivated by Greed was in the Billiard Room at 11:00 PM."
     - Candlestick at \( (0, 0) \) at 11:00 PM.
     - Greed suspect in Billiard Room (Colonel Mustard at \( (1, 0) \)).
     - **Conclusion:** No contradiction.

   - For each move in the Detective’s path, manually calculate the Manhattan distance.
     - **Example:**
       - Professor Plum at \( (0, 0) \) at 11:00 PM to \( (2, 1) \) at 12:30 AM.

1. **Disjunction Validation (P or Q):**

   - No other suspect is mentioned in the Trophy Room at that time.

### Hard Audit on the Deduction

#### Disjunction Validation

1. **P or Q - Mr. Boddy was in the room just west of Professor Plum at 12:30 AM.**
   - True (Mr. Boddy was in the Fountain at 12:30 AM)
   - No contradiction.

2. **P or Q - Mr. Boddy was in the room just west of Professor Plum at 12:30 AM (Logically implied by the timeline).**
   - True (Mr. Boddy was in the Fountain at 12:30 AM)
   - No contradiction.

#### Biconditional Audit

1. **P if and only if Q - The Horseshoe was at the Fountain at 11:00 PM if and only if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM.**
   - Both conditions are not met (false/true or true/false)
   - Logical Error: Biconditional Not Met.

2. **P if and only if Q - The Horseshoe was in the Cloak Room at 12:30 AM if and only if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 11:30 PM.**
   - Both conditions are not met (false/true or true/false)
   - Logical Error: Biconditional Not Met.

#### Contradiction Search

1. **"Mr. Boddy was in the room just west of Professor Plum at 12:30 AM" and "The Lead Pipe was in the room just west of Colonel Mustard at 12:30 AM."**
   - Contradiction: The room just west of Professor Plum (Fountain) cannot be the same as the room just west of Colonel Mustard (Cloak Room) at the same time.
   - POLITICAL VIOLATION: BRANCH IS CONTRADICTORY.

#### Manhattan Falsification

1. **"Professor Plum was in the Library at 11:00 PM or a suspect motivated by Greed was in the Billiard Room at 11:00 PM."**
   - Professor Plum was in the Library at 11:00 PM (0,0)
     - Move to Billiard Room (0,1) if not in the Library.
     - Manhattan distance (|0-0| + |1-0|) = 1

2. **"Mr. Boddy was at the Fountain at 12:30 AM or Miss Scarlet was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM."**
   - Miss Scarlet was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM (2,0)
     - Move to Fountain (1,0)
     - Manhattan distance (|2-1| + |0-0|) = 1

3. **"The Horseshoe was at the Fountain at 11:30 PM if and only if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM."**
   - False condition (Horseshoe was in Cloak Room at 11:30 PM and Poison was not in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM)
   - No violation.

4. **"The Horseshoe was in the same room as Colonel Mustard at 11:00 PM."**
   - If Horseshoe was in the Cloak Room at 11:00 PM and the Poison was not in the Trophy Room, then Colonel Mustard must have moved to the Cloak Room.
   - No violation (if true, then both conditions are true).

#### Final Verdict

- **Logical Error: Biconditional Not Met** (twice)
- **Logical Error: Branch Is Contradictory**
- **PHYSICAL VIOLATION: BRANCH IS CONTRADICTORY**

[33mDetective[0m (to Agency):

### Step-by-Step Analysis

#### Step 1: Identify Key Clues and Assign Initial Positions

- **11:00 PM:**
  - **Mrs. Peacock was in the Studio at 11:00 PM or the Lead Pipe was in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM.**
  - **The Candlestick was in the Library at 11:00 PM or a suspect motivated by Greed was in the Billiard Room at 11:00 PM.**
  - **The Lead Pipe was in the room just north of Mrs. Peacock at 11:00 PM.**
  - **Professor Plum was in the room just east of the Poison at 11:30 PM.**
  - **The Horseshoe was in the same room as Colonel Mustard at 11:00 PM.**

From the initial clues:
- **Mrs. Peacock** is in a position: Let's assume she is in the Studio (0,1) at 11:00 PM.
- **Lead Pipe** is in the Trophy Room (1,1).
- **Mrs. Peacock** and **Lead Pipe** condition is true, so Mrs. Peacock cannot be in the Studio at 11:00 PM. Therefore, **the Lead Pipe is in the Trophy Room at 11:00 PM.**
- **Horseshoe** is in the same room as Colonel Mustard at 11:00 PM. Let's assume **Colonel Mustard** is in and around the Horseshoe.
- **Mr. Boddy killed with the Candlestick** suggests the Candlestick is in the Library (0,0) at 11:00 PM.

Now we have:
- **Mrs. Peacock (Studio 0,1)**
- **Lead Pipe (Trophy Room 1,1)**
- **Colonel Mustard (with Horseshoe)**
- **Candlestick (Library 0,0)**

#### Step 2: Analyze 11:30 PM Movements

- **Mr. Boddy and Colonel Mustard were at the Fountain together at least once.**
- **Professor Plum was in the room just east of the Poison at 11:30 PM.**

Let's assume:
- **Colonel Mustard** and **Mr. Boddy** move to the Fountain (1,0) at 11:30 PM.
- **Professor Plum** moves to the room just east of the Poison (0,1), which is the Studio (0,1). This is a valid move because it is orthogonal.

Now we have:
- **Mrs. Peacock (Studio 0,1)**
- **Lead Pipe (Trophy Room 1,1)**
- **Colonel Mustard (Fountain 1,0)**

- **Professor Plum (Studio 0,1)**

#### Step 3: Analyze 12:00 AM Movements

- **Mrs. Peacock was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.**
- **A suspect motivated by Jealousy was in the same room as the Lead Pipe at 11:00 PM.**
  - **Colonel Mustard is motivated by Jealousy.**
- **The Horseshoe was in the same room as Mrs. Peacock at 12:30 AM.**

Let's update:
- **Mrs. Peacock moves to the Cloak Room (2,0) at 12:00 AM.**
- **Colonel Mustard remains in the Fountain.**

Now we have:
- **Mrs. Peacock (Cloak Room 2,0)**
- **Lead Pipe (Trophy Room 1,1)**
- **Colonel Mustard (Fountain 1,0)**
- **Mr. Boddy (Fountain 1,0)**
- **Professor Plum (Studio 0,1)**

#### Step 4: Analyze 12:30 AM Movements

- **Monsieur Brunette was in the Billiard Room at 11:30 PM.**
- **The Lead Pipe and Miss Scarlet were in the Cloak Room together at least once.**
- **The Horseshoe was at the Fountain at 11:00 PM if and only if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 12:30 AM.**
- **The Poison was in the same room as Monsieur Brunette at 12:30 AM.**

Assume:
- **Monsieur Brunette** is in the Billiard Room (0,1) at 12:30 AM.
- **Poison** in the Trophy Room (1,1) at 12:30 AM.
- **Horseshoe at the Fountain (1,0). This condition is met if the Poison was in the Trophy Room at 12:30 AM.**

Now we have:
- **Mrs. Peacock (Cloak Room 2,0)**
- **Lead Pipe (Trophy Room 1,1)**
- **Colonel Mustard (Fountain 1,0)**
- **Mr. Boddy (Fountain 1,0)**

- **Horseshoe (Fountain 1,0)**

#### Step 5: Identify 12:00 AM Room (Murder)

- **Mr. Boddy was killed with the Candlestick at 12:00 AM.**
- **Mrs. Peacock was in the Cloak Room at 12:00 AM.**
- **A suspect motivated by Jealousy (Colonel Mustard) was in the same room as the Lead Pipe at 11:00 PM.**

Given:
- **Professor Plum** is in the Studio (0,1) and **Colonel Mustard** is in the Fountain (1,0).
- **Candlestick (Library 0,0)**, **Lead Pipe (Trophy Room 1,1)**, **Colonel Mustard (Fountain 1,0)**, and **Mrs. Peacock (Cloak Room 2,0)** are logical.

#### Step 6: Check for Murder Location

- **Library (0,0):** Possible, but no strong evidence.
- **Fountain (1,0):** Possible, but no strong evidence.
- **Cloak Room (2,0):** No strong evidence.
- **Trophy Room (1,1):** Possibility emerges because:
